# CODE3B — Algo Trader (EMA + RSI + Range)

## Project Ka Kaam
Multi-strategy live/paper algo trader jo Dhan API pe orders deta hai.
Web dashboard se control hota hai — koi command line nahi.

## Folder Structure (2026-07-09 refactor — PADHO)

Root pe pehle 60+ flat `.py` the; ab logical folders me organized hain. **Imports NAHI
badle** — `_paths.py` (root) har entrypoint me sabse pehle import hota hai aur har folder
ko `sys.path` pe daal deta hai, to flat `import risk_gate` / `import dhan_master` /
`import range_trader` waise hi kaam karta hai chahe file kahin bhi ho.

```
trader_dashboard.py  monitor_daemon.py  health_check.py   ← entrypoints (root, systemd yahin se)
_paths.py            ← sys.path bootstrap (har directly-run file iske baad hi moved-module import kare)
_core/    risk_gate order_store smart_order broker_sync execution_gateway webhook_executor
          strategy_safety daily_state payoff              ← RMS + order/execution money-path
_data/    dhan_master dhan_feed dhan_rate_limiter kite_rate_limiter ltp_poller
          shared_ltp_cache shared_candle_cache universe fno_universe   ← broker/data plumbing
_ops/     auto_data_downloader download_equity_history download_nifty50 export_trade_log
          rate_limit_verify deploy_vps sync_*                          ← standalone ops scripts
strategies/
  backtest/  pluggable backtest strategies (evaluate/backtest contract) + user_*_v{n}
  live/      LIVE trader loops (range_trader, universe_trader, 01_rsi_v1, nifty_ema_trader)
             + range_config.json + NEW_STRATEGY_CHECKLIST.md   ← (was _TRADERS/)
  lab/       AI-built strategy lab (per-strategy folders + Monte-Carlo/optimize/reports — see lab/README.md)
  base.py  custom_rule_engine.py  __init__.py   ← strategy infra (load() is backward-compat)
_TOOLS/  _CHARTING/  _PINE/  _ADR/  _DEPLOY/  brokers/  templates/  static/  data/   ← unchanged
_DEV/tests/  _DEV/mockups/   ← test + mockup files
scratch/                ← .gitignore me hai, PAR do alag cheezein ek naam ke neeche:
  scratch/nifty_trend/  ← 🔴 JUNK NAHI. Research engine — har shipped strategy ke validated
                          Sharpe/net yahin se bante hain (run_hunt/bs_option/charges/
                          intraday_engine), _core/payoff.py isi se bs_option import karta hai,
                          RESULTS_SCHEMA.md + BS_OPTION_SIM.md ka ghar. git-tracked (force-add),
                          aur 2026-07-16 se architecture_audit ke andar bhi.
  scratch/<baaki>       ← asli junk (check_*.py, delete_*.py, one-off probes). Untracked.
  ⚠️ Naya file scratch/nifty_trend/ me banao to `git add -f` karna padega — folder .gitignore
     me hai, warna `git add` chupchaap kuch nahi karega aur wo file VPS tak kabhi nahi jaayegi.
```

**Rules:**
- **Nayi strategy?** backtest logic → `strategies/backtest/`; live trader loop → `strategies/live/`;
  AI experiment (build+optimize+MC) → `strategies/lab/<name>/`.
- **Directly-run naya script** (subprocess/daemon/CLI) jo project module import kare → `import _paths`
  add karo (root pe: `import _paths`; subfolder me: pehle root ko `sys.path` pe daalo, phir `import _paths`).
- **Moved module me path banate waqt** `Path(__file__).parent...` mat maano ki root hai — folder-depth
  ke hisaab se `.parent` count karo (data/config/db sab project ROOT ke neeche hain).
- Full audit + phased plan: `REFACTOR_PLAN.md`. VPS deploy checklist: `VPS_MIGRATION.md`.

## Files

> **🐞 Koi bug/weird-P&L diagnose karne se PEHLE: `LESSONS.md` padho.** Recurring traps ki
> indexed library hai (₹0-price phantom fills, DH-904 rate-limit, galat-process-blame, Python
> 3.8 VPS crash, deploy gotchas, UI fingerprint-skip). Bug fix karke jo dobara aa sakta hai —
> wahan ek entry add karo. Goal: **ek bug do baar diagnose na karna.** (`ARCHITECTURE_LOG.md` =
> "kya badla" changelog; `LESSONS.md` = "kya seekha / permanent guard".)
>
> **📘 Pine→Python validation? PEHLE YEH PADHO:** `ACCURACY SCORE CLAUD/VALIDATION_PLAYBOOK.md`
> — reusable methodology + all findings (fill convention, Wilder ATR, pyramiding=0,
> consistent-log export, etc.). Pichli baar pura din laga; yeh playbook se turant pick hoga.
> Tool: `validate_strategy.py` (`--signals <log>` to score, `--debug DATE` to trace).
> Best result: **90.2% exact / 93% entry** (Range Chain vs TradingView, NIFTY).
>
> **🧭 Koi NAYI live strategy (`strategies/live/*.py`) likhne se PEHLE: `strategies/live/NEW_STRATEGY_CHECKLIST.md`
> padho.** Copy-paste-ready RMS-safe entry/exit templates + har wo galti jo yahan bite kar chuki
> (RMS-blind order_store gap, manual-close phantom BUY, ₹0-price fill, `symbols` string-parse,
> default-arg `.keys()` crash, dashboard `STRATEGIES` grep-mismatch, restart-orphan). Critical
> Rule 6/8 ka full practical version — ek galti do baar na ho.
>
> **📊 Koi strategy ka BACKTEST banao / uska RESULT dikhao? PEHLE YE 2 PADHO (MANDATORY):**
> `scratch/nifty_trend/RESULTS_SCHEMA.md` + `scratch/nifty_trend/BS_OPTION_SIM.md`.
> User chahta hai ki HAR strategy ka result **ek hi reusable dashboard** (`scratch/nifty_trend/
> dashboard_intraday.html`) mein isi format mein dikhe — plain nayi HTML mat banao, `results.js`
> is schema mein emit karo, wahi template render kar lega. Aur backtest ka P&L/tax **option-premium
> based** hona chahiye (ATM CE/PE ka Black-Scholes premium at entry+exit + real Zerodha
> `calc_charges`), **spot-notional nahi** — asli expired-weekly option data milta nahi (LESSONS
> TRAP #100), isliye BS se simulate karo. 1x no-leverage default, significance-gate (p<0.05),
> RMS-overlay validation deploy se pehle. Ye do docs = strategy-results ka permanent contract;
> user ko baar-baar dena na pade — yahin se utha lo.
>
> **3-PASS PIPELINE (mandatory display model):** har result **teen alag passes** mein dikhe, ek hi
> dashboard ke andar PASS toggle se — **① Instrument** (raw signal spot P&L, no RMS/options) →
> **② + RMS** (same trades + daily loss/profit caps) → **③ + Black-Scholes** (pass-② trades ATM
> CE/PE premium mein repriced — asli deployable P&L). Combos `results.js` mein `"<pass>|<period>"`
> keyed (`bs|full`, `rms|oos`…). **Reference producer + sabse aasan reuse = `scratch/nifty_trend/
> run_hunt.py --name <slug>`** — ye khud screen→optimize(min(train,oos), TRAP #103)→significance→
> 3-pass→BS reprice karke `runs/<slug>/` (results.js+index.html+meta.json) likhta hai aur
> `runs/index.json` mein append karta hai → Strategy Lab hub (`hub.html`) usse auto-list kar leta
> hai. BS engine = `bs_option.py` (pricing+charges+σ+lot). **Har strategy apne `runs/<slug>/` folder
> mein** — dhundhna aasan, dashboard reusable.

| `strategy_safety.py` | **Shared "backoffice" layer every strategy's entry goes through** — `gate_entry()` (RMS pre-trade gate: gating_status/drawdown/**live liquidity**/concentration/capital/broker-funds, one call) + `check_contract_liquidity()` (live spread%/volume/OI any-2-of-3 gate on the EXACT option contract, replaces needing an ever-growing static stock whitelist — RMS Risk tab "📊 Live Liquidity Filter" toggle) + `compute_hedge_target()` (auto-hedge contract resolution, reads RMS Risk tab). Does NOT place orders — placement stays strategy-specific (raw REST, `smart_order.execute()`, whatever). See "Building a new strategy" above + LESSONS.md TRAP #15/#17. |
| `dhan_master.py` | Daily dhan scrip master download + Option contract resolver |

See LESSONS.md TRAP #13. |
| `kite_rate_limiter.py` | Cross-process rate-gate for Kite Connect calls — same sqlite token-bucket pattern as `dhan_rate_limiter.py`, separate DB (different account/API, different quota). |
| `dhan_rate_limiter.py` | **Cross-process Dhan rate-limiter + priority gate** (sqlite, no extra dep). Every real Dhan call (candle/ltp/order/margin) from every process routes through `acquire(priority)` before hitting the account-wide ~1 req/sec limit. `"order"` priority always gets a reserved slot — never starved behind LTP/candle polling. `note_429()` triggers an 8s cooldown where non-order traffic stops entirely so the account recovers without blocking new orders. See LESSONS.md TRAP #2 (v2 guard). |
| `dhan_feed.py` | **Live WebSocket tick feed** — background thread keeps an in-memory `LIVE` dict (best bid/ask/LTP/OI per sec_id) fed by `dhanhq.DhanFeed` (v2, Full packets). REWRITTEN 2026-06-27 — was importing `DhanContext`/`MarketFeed`, which don't exist in the installed `dhanhq==2.0.2`; feed never connected at all (silently, then loudly after the 2026-06-24 logging fix). See LESSONS.md TRAP #11. Public API unchanged: `start(creds, sec_tuples)`, `add(sec_tuple)`, `get_quote(sec_id)`. Used by `smart_order.py` (marketable-limit pricing), `pos_monitor_loop`/`/api/ltp-stream` (Open Positions live LTP — was already wired to prefer this feed, just never benefited until the rewrite), and `/api/option-ltp` (Quick Order widget, wired in same session). |

Script resolution `health_check.TRADER_SCRIPTS` se (Rule 6B), contract-check `(df,cfg)`/`(token,cid,...)` — legacy engines gracefully N/A. **USI SHAAM chalao** (Dhan intraday history chhoti). `run_for(sid,date)` importable (eod_report isi se). |

> `project_code3b_vps_git_sync`). GitHub `arsalanali36/algo-trader` = dono side ki single
> source of truth. VPS repo REAL path `/root/ARSALAN/CODE3B- TV BACKTEST ENGINE`
> (`/root/CODE3B...` = symlink). Runtime files (`nifty_config.json`/`data/config.json`/

**Normal flow:**
```bash
# LOCAL pe kaam kiya:
git add <files> && git commit -m "..." && git push origin master

# VPS pe deploy (scp ki jagah) — ssh karke:
ssh -i "C:/Users/arsal/.ssh/khazana_ed25519" root@72.61.173.32 \
  "cd '/root/ARSALAN/CODE3B- TV BACKTEST ENGINE' && git pull origin master && systemctl restart algo-dashboard"
```
- **VPS working tree dirty ho (local modifications)?** poora upgrade: `git fetch && git reset --hard origin/master` (⚠️ VPS-side uncommitted changes uda dega — pehle `git status` dekho; gitignored config/DB safe rehte hain). Ya surgical: `git checkout origin/master -- <file>`.
- **VPS pe live-fix kiya?** wahin `git commit && git push` (deploy-key se), phir LOCAL pe `git pull` — warna wahi drift wapas.
- Deploy ke baad hamesha: strategy PIDs before==after check (`KillMode=process` → dashboard restart children ko nahi maarta), md5/`git log -1` local==VPS.

> **Purana scp/`_ops/deploy_vps.py` tareeka** ab fallback-only (git down ho tabhi). VPS git repo
> banne se pehle wahi standard tha — commit history me use dikhega.

## Strategy Variations & Options Trading

Ab system mein aap ek strategy ke **multiple variations** (jaise `ema_v1`, `ema_v2`, `rsi_v1`) alag alag configurations ke saath ek hi waqt pe chala sakte hain. Har variation ek isolated Python process ki tarah chalta hai.

- **Options Support**: Config grid mein Instrument = "Options" select karke Strike Offset (-3 to +3) de sakte hain.
- **PE/CE Selling**: Agar Long signal aata hai toh PE sell hoga, Short signal pe CE sell hoga. `dhan_master.py` dynamically live ATM strike calculate karke scrip nikalta hai.
- **Config Storage**: Saari configs ek single file `nifty_config.json` mein store hoti hain under keys like `ema_v1`, `range_v2`.

```
/root/CODE3B- TV BACKTEST ENGINE/   ← VPS pe (purana /root/code4 nahi)
├── logs/                 ← Har variation ki separate log file (e.g. ema_v1.log)
├── nifty_config.json     ← All configurations (hot-reload)
├── data/                 
│   ├── config.json       ← Dhan JWT + client_id (SECRET — gitignored)
│   └── api-scrip-master.csv ← Dhan Options symbols list
└── results/
    ├── YYYY-MM-DD.txt    ← Daily P&L summary
    └── master_log.json   ← All-time P&L history
```

## Critical Rules — Kabhi Mat Bhoolna

### 1. IPv4 Force (DH-905 fix)
VPS pe IPv6 default hoti hai — Dhan reject karta hai. Har trader file ke top mein yeh hona CHAHIYE:
```python
import socket
_orig_gai = socket.getaddrinfo
def _v4(h, p, f=0, t=0, pr=0, fl=0):
    return _orig_gai(h, p, socket.AF_INET, t, pr, fl)
socket.getaddrinfo = _v4
```

### 2. Candles — Dhan intraday API only
All candle data comes from Dhan `/v2/charts/intraday` (sec_id from `dhan_master.get_equity_info()`).
Index spot price from Dhan `/v2/marketfeed/ltp` with `IDX_I` segment (sec_id 13=NIFTY, 25=BANKNIFTY).

### 3. correlationId = strategy prefix
Har order ka ek specific correlation ID hota hai jisme strategy version prefix hota hai: `EMA_V1_NIFTY_<timestamp>`.
Issey dashboard P&L parse kar pata hai.

### 4. Dhan token — rozana update
JWT token har 24 ghante mein expire hota hai.
Dashboard ke **Control tab** mein token paste karo → Save.

### 5. Auto Scheduler
Subah 9:10 par `trader_dashboard.py` apne aap saare active variations ko paper mode mein start kar deta hai, aur 15:30 par stop.

### 6. RMS is SUPREME — har strategy order_store mein likhe (warna RMS andha)
RMS (`risk_gate.py`) ki guardrails — unified daily-loss breaker
(`effective_daily_loss_cap`/`daily_loss_breached`, always-on default ₹5000) aur
capital allocation — ko **koi strategy bypass nahi kar sakti**. Ye breaker dono
karta hai: nayi entry block + `pos_monitor_loop` se open positions ka force
squareoff. Per-strategy SL/target iske **neeche** independent hain (jaldi exit
kar sakte hain, par RMS cap se aage kabhi nahi).
**HARD RULE for every strategy (current + future):** har order entry/exit
`smart_order.execute()` ya `order_store.record()` ke through hi jaaye — RMS sirf
wahi positions dekhta hai jo order_store mein hain. Jo strategy seedha broker pe
order daale (in-memory dicts + log se, order_store mein nahi) wo **RMS-blind**
hai aur use live nahi chalana chahiye jab tak order_store recording add na ho.
(`01_rsi_v1.py`=rsi_v1 already order_store-wired hai — `smart_order.execute()` +
`strategy_safety.gate_entry()` ke through — is note ka "abhi RMS-blind" naam
2026-07-03 tak stale tha, `nifty_ema_trader.py`=ema_v1 ka status verify na hone
tak isi list mein maano.) Nayi strategy banate waqt: pehle order_store recording
wire karo.

### 6B. Naya code likhne se pehle: DUPLICATE mat karo, EXTEND karo

Ye repo mein baar-baar ek hi bug-family bani hai: kisi ne ek naya function
likh diya jo already kahin exist karta tha (indicator, risk-check, order-call,
backtest-simulation) — aur do "sach" ban gaye jo aage chal kar diverge ho gaye.
Isliye har naya code (chahe khud likho ya AI se likhwao) is order mein jaaye:

1. **Indicator chahiye?** (EMA/RSI/ATR/VWAP/pivot/koi bhi) →
   `_CHARTING/indicators.py` ka `INDICATOR_REGISTRY` aur `_CHARTING/zones.py`
   check karo. Wahan hai → wahi import karo. Nahi hai aur generic hai →
   wahin add karo, apni file ke andar inline mat likho.

2. **Order place/cancel/chase karna hai?** → `smart_order.execute()` hi use
   karo. `execute_signal()` gateway ban chuka ho (dekho neeche) to usi se
   jao. Broker object pe seedha `place_order()`/`cancel_order()` kabhi mat
   bulao strategy file se.

3. **Risk/capital/concentration/drawdown check karna hai?** → `risk_gate.py`
   / `strategy_safety.gate_entry()` ke through hi jao. Inline check kabhi
   mat likho.

4. **Backtest/simulation code likh rahe ho?** → agar `execute_signal()`
   gateway ban chuka hai, uska `mode="backtest"` use karo — apna alag
   capital/risk simulation mat likho. Agar gateway abhi nahi bana, user ko
   explicitly batao ki "ye backtest path live risk rules follow nahi kar
   raha" — chup-chaap koi shortcut mat lo.

5. **State jo restart ke baad bhi chahiye** (peaks, pending actions, locks)
   → disk-persist karo, `_pending_group_close`/`_kf_state` jaisa pattern
   (`trader_dashboard.py` mein dekho). Plain in-memory dict/list mat rakho.

6. **Commit karne se pehle**: `_TOOLS/architecture_audit.py` khud chalao
   (ya commit karte hi pre-commit hook automatically chalayega). Agar FAIL
   aaye, commit se pehle fix karo — "chhota sa fix hai" soch kar skip mat
   karo. **Fresh clone / nayi machine / VPS pe** hook install karna zaroori
   hai (`.git/hooks/` tracked nahi hoti) — ek baar `bash scripts/setup_hooks.sh`
   chalao, phir har commit pe audit khud chalega.

**Kyun**: ye repo ka pura design intent hi yehi hai ki "signal generate karna"
aur "signal ko safely execute/plot/backtest karna" do alag layers hon — pehli
layer (strategy files) sirf entry/exit condition likhe, doosri layer
(gateway/charting/risk_gate) baaki sab sambhale. Is separation ko todna
matlab purane saare TRAPs (#71-#94+) wapas possible banana.

### 6C. Bade architectural decision pe ADR likho

`LESSONS.md` sirf BUGS/TRAPS ke liye hai (reactive — jo hua uske baad likha
gaya). Koi bhi **bada structural/architectural decision** — chahe bug se
related ho ya na ho — `_ADR/` folder mein alag se likho:

- File naam: `_ADR/ADR-00X-short-title.md` (number badhta jaye)
- Format: Context (problem kya thi) → Decision (kya choose kiya) →
  Consequence (kya trade-off accept kiya)
- Chhoti rakho — 10-15 min mein padhne layak, poora essay nahi

**Kab likhni hai**: jab bhi koi choice ki jaye jo future mein "ye kyun aisa
kiya tha" wala sawaal utha sakti hai — jaise ek naya shared gateway banane ka
decision, kisi do duplicate versions mein se "sach" kaun sa hai ye decide
karna, ya koi bhi design jo alternative options ke against choose hua ho.

Task complete karte waqt khud check karo: "kya isme koi aisa decision tha jo
future mein confusing lagega agar likha na jaye?" Agar haan, ADR likhna kaam
ka hissa hai, extra step nahi.

### 6D. Automation / `/loop` — LIVE-ORDER-PATH ko KABHI touch nahi (Task 5)

Koi bhi recurring loop / scheduled agent / unattended automation (jaise
`.claude/commands/architecture-audit-loop.md`, `eod-report-review.md`,
`hunt-pipeline.md`) **kabhi bhi** in files ko edit / auto-fix / stage / deploy
**nahi** kar sakta:

`_core/execution_gateway.py`, `_core/webhook_executor.py`, `_core/smart_order.py`,
`_core/risk_gate.py`, `_core/strategy_safety.py`, `_core/order_store.py` — aur
aam taur pe `_core/` ka koi bhi order-path file, plus `nifty_config.json` ka koi
bhi `active`/`mode` flag.

Loops sirf **research / audit / reporting** ke liye hain — real order place kar
sakne wale kisi bhi code ko chhoona **manual-only** hai: ek Claude Code session,
ek waqt, explicit review + explicit approval commit se pehle. Loop ko aisa
finding mile jo in files mein ho → sirf **draft/text** mein user ko batao, file
mat likho. Ye rule tab bhi laagu hai jab loop "chhota sa safe fix" lagta ho.

Har loop mein yeh rails bhi honi chahiye (dekho command files): idle-aware skip
(active manual session ho to ruk jao), budget cap (`max_runtime_min`),
`data/loop_activity.log` mein ek-line-per-run, `max_consecutive_failures=3`,
aur kabhi auto-commit nahi.

### 6E. SIGNAL ek hi jagah — backtest aur live WAHI code chalayein (ADR-010 / TRAP #153)

Ek strategy live ISLIYE deploy hoti hai kyunki uska **backtest ka number trusted hai**.
Wo trust tabhi valid hai jab **live wahi entry-signal fire kare jo backtest ne naapa**.
Mahino tak aisa nahi tha: har strategy ke paas signal ki **do alag Python copy** thi —
backtest engine (`scratch/nifty_trend/intraday_engine.design_signals`) aur har live trader
ke andar apni **inline copy**. Wo silently drift kar gayin (`orb_v1` 33% match, `dvert`
**opposite-sign P&L**; OR-boundary `<` vs `<=`, prev-bar vs current-bar ATR crossover).

**Rule:** signal ki **ek hi implementation** `strategies/signals/*.py` mein rahegi
(`orb.py`, `chain_zone.py`); backtest usse **delegate** kare, har live trader usse **call**
kare (`orb.orb_signal_last` / `chain_zone.chain_zone_signal_last`). Sirf ATR-stop /
order-sizing trader mein. **Live trader mein signal ki inline copy KABHI mat likho.**

- Naya signal? → pehle `strategies/signals/<name>.py`, backtest + live dono usse call karein,
  phir `_DEV/tests/test_<name>_single_source.py` guard (backtest==shared bit-identical +
  live==backtest fired bars).
- **Commit-time guard** `INLINE-SIGNAL` (`_TOOLS/architecture_audit.py`) inline ORB/zone ko
  block karta hai; escape `# inline-signal-ok: <wajah>` sirf documented not-yet-migrated file.
- **Honest boundary:** sirf spot-candle-series signal literally share ho sakta hai. VRP
  (IV-rank live-premium vs lake), BankNifty (alag store), RSI/EMA (alag design), ARS_CHAIN
  (Pine 90.2% validated) — alag situations, inpe zabardasti "100% match" mat banao.

### 7. Kabhi ₹0-price pe REAL fill record mat karo (LESSONS.md TRAP #1)
Option premium fetch fail ho (DH-904 rate-limit) to `price = 0` record karna ek
real fill ke roop me **P&L corrupt karta hai** (SELL@₹0 → close@real = jhootha bada
loss/profit → RMS breaker trip → asli legs squareoff). Yeh bug 4 baar laut chuka.
**Rule:** premium na mile to entry **SKIP karo** (₹0 record mat karo) — `shared_ltp_cache`
→ direct Dhan (backoff) → stale cache try karke bhi na mile to caller ko skip karwao.
`order_store.record()` me central tripwire hai jo ₹0 real-fill pe loud ⚠️ log karta hai —
us warning ko kabhi ignore mat karo. Detail + fast-detect query: `LESSONS.md` TRAP #1.

### 8. Naya strategy banate waqt — yeh 4 cheezein har baar (LESSONS.md TRAP #15)
Saari safety/RMS logic ek baar `strategy_safety.py` mein bana di gayi hai — naye strategy
mein use **dohrana nahi**, sirf call karna hai. Koi bhi naya strategy file banao, yeh checklist:

1. **Order place karne ke liye** `smart_order.execute()` use karo (broker class ke through —
   `from brokers import get_broker`), raw `requests.post()` Dhan/Kite ko seedha mat maaro.
   Isse automatically milta hai: rate-limiting (`dhan_rate_limiter`/`kite_rate_limiter`),
   async order-confirm (LESSONS.md TRAP #14), `order_store` recording.
2. **Entry se pehle** `strategy_safety.gate_entry(strategy_id, symbol, lots, lot_size,
   est_price, side, sec_id=..., mode=..., broker=...)` call karo — yeh gating_status +
   drawdown + concentration + capital (size-down) + broker-funds sab ek call mein karta hai.
   `ok=False` aaye to entry skip karo, `qty` returned value use karo (sized-down ho sakta hai).
3. **Naked option SELL hai to hedge ke liye** `strategy_safety.compute_hedge_target(strategy_id,
   symbol, spot_price, option_type, sell_offset, quote_fn=...)` call karo — RMS Risk tab ke
   Min Strikes/Max Premium fields automatically isi se aate hain, kahin alag se config nahi
   chahiye. Resolve hone ke baad apne normal place-order tareeke se BUY bhej do.
4. **`order_store.record()` mein likhna zaroori hai** (Critical Rule 6 — RMS sirf wahi positions
   dekhta hai). `smart_order.execute()` use karo to yeh automatic hai.

`strategies/live/range_trader.py` (raw REST) aur `webhook_executor.py` (broker-class) — dono alag
order-placement style use karte hain par dono `strategy_safety.py` ke sirf yeh 2 functions
call karte hain. Naya strategy chahe kaisa bhi order bheje, isi pattern follow karo.

### 9. Har exit order pe reason TAG karo — kabhi khaali mat chhodo (LESSONS.md TRAP #88/#91)
2026-07-03 ko do alag strategies (`range_trader.py`, `01_rsi_v1.py`) mein EXACT same gap
mila — dono ka apna exit-signal logic apna "reason" compute + LOG karta tha, par kabhi
`order_store` mein TAG nahi karta tha (`smart_order.execute(..., is_exit=True)` call mein
`extra_tags` hi missing tha). Result: dashboard ka "Exit Reason" column blank raha for
har strategy-driven exit — sirf `pos_monitor_loop`'s apne SL/TP/EOD exits already tag
karte the (wo alag code path hai, bug wahan nahi tha). Audit ke baad pata chala: 119
purani completed trades (sabhi strategies mila ke) is exact gap ki wajah se blank thi.

**Naya strategy/exit-path banate/edit karte waqt:**
- Har exit call (`smart_order.execute()` ya `place_order()`) mein `extra_tags=[reason]`
  zaroor pass karo — `reason` chahe simple string ho (`"RSI_MIDLINE_EXIT"`) ya dynamic
  (`f"ATR_TRAILING"`), koi bhi exit reason-less nahi jaana chahiye.
- Naya reason string `order_store.py`'s `_EXIT_REASON_PREFIXES` list mein bhi add karo
  (warna `_exit_reason()` usko recognize nahi karega, tag hoke bhi column blank dikhega)
  — aur `templates/index.html`'s `_exitReasonBadge()` mein ek proper emoji+color badge
  bhi de do (warna plain gray text fallback dikhega).
- **Agar koi missing-reason gap MILE (existing strategy mein), sirf code fix mat karo —
  historical blank rows ko bhi backfill karo** jahan reason confidently pata ho (e.g. us
  strategy ka poora git history check karke — agar sirf EK possible reason value kabhi
  bana hi nahi, to backfill safe hai). Jahan ambiguous ho (multiple possible reasons,
  ya retired/unclear strategy jiska current config hi nahi bacha), wahan GUESS mat karo —
  blank hi chhodo aur user ko clearly batao kyun confident nahi ho (LESSONS.md TRAP #91's
  backfill: 71/119 confidently backfilled — `ARS_CHAIN_V1`→`ATR_TRAILING`, `rsi_v1`→
  `RSI_MIDLINE_EXIT`, `source=manual`→`MANUAL_CLOSE` — baaki 48 jaanbhoojkar chhode gaye).

### 10. 🛑 BACKTEST-FIDELITY — jo backtest me nahi tha, wo live me daloge to result jhooth (user-set 2026-07-13)

Har strategy live ISLIYE deploy hoti hai kyunki uska 3-pass backtest ka number (Sharpe/net/
win%) trusted hai. Us strategy ke **entry / exit / risk behaviour** me koi bhi cheez add/enable
karna jo **us backtest me thi hi nahi** — trailing SL, extra profit-lock, aggressive
Default-TSL, koi RMS cap jo bite kare, naya filter, badla exit — **live/paper ko backtest se
diverge kar deta hai**, aur jo validated result user dekh raha hai wo **ab hold nahi karta**
(fiction ban jaata hai).

**Isliye aisa koi bhi tweak likhne/enable karne se PEHLE:**
1. **RUKO aur user ko pehle bolo** (plain Hinglish): "ye tweak backtest me nahi tha — enable
   karoge to jo result dikh raha hai wo nahi rahega." Chup-chaap "on" ship mat karo.
2. Do imandaar raaste har baar offer karo:
   - **(a) Tweak chahiye** → pehle **backtest engine me bhi wahi tweak daalo, re-run karo**,
     naya validated number nikaalo, TAB live enable → ab live == backtest.
   - **(b) Dikhta number hold rakhna hai** → tweak OFF / mat daalo. Strategy as-validated rahe.
3. Agar tweak ship karna hi ho to **DISABLED** ship karo (jaise backspread trailing lock,
   `trail_enabled:false`) + saaf bolo ki re-backtest ke bina enable nahi hoga.

**Same root as:** task-69 aggressive-Default-TSL (backtest me nahi thi → paper data corrupt) +
"caps set but backtest not re-run" (per-strategy caps live hain par hub Sharpe dummy-cap pe
computed). Sab ek hi jad: **live config ≠ backtested config → number jhooth.** Memory:
`feedback-backtest-fidelity-rule`.

## 🧨 PRE-MORTEM — money/positions/orders touch karne wale HAR naye feature se pehle (2026-07-02 se mandatory)

LESSONS.md ke 81 TRAPs ka nichod: bugs alag the, FAILURE SHAPES bar-bar wahi hain.
Naya feature likhne se PEHLE har shape pe yes/no + 1-line reasoning likho (chat me hi,
essay nahi). Koi "yes" mila to code se pehle guard design karo. Ship ke BAAD same list
dobara chalao — is baar har answer ke saath ek asli runtime log line ya test-output ka
saboot, "code sahi lag raha hai" nahi.

| # | Failure shape | Classic TRAPs |
|---|---------------|---------------|
| 1 | **Stale-state action** — position/order ki state fresh recheck ke bina "abhi bhi sach" maan kar order fire karna | #28 #44 #51 #58 #62 #73 #74 #75 |
| 2 | **Built ≠ wired ≠ verified** — feature ban gaya lekin kabhi call/start/render nahi hua, ya kabhi live data ke saath verify nahi hua | #11 #12 #17 #65, alert-banner (07-02) |
| 3 | **RAM-only state, restart pe gone** — dict/var jo restart survive karna chahiye tha lekin disk pe nahi tha | #28 #38 #52 #55 #57 #76, _pos_peaks, _pending_group_close, kill_floor_state |
| 4 | **Duplicate logic, aadha fix** — same logic 2 jagah, fix sirf ek me laga; ya per-X variant ne aggregate branch copy-paste kar liya bina adjust kiye | #16 #26-addendum #27 #77 #78 #80 |
| 5 | **Risk-check silently fails open, ya writeup me diagnose hone ke baad bhi actual line fix nahi hui** | #56 #72 #81, _trailing_lock_fired_today |
| 6 | **Shared resource, independent hitters** — har process apni taraf se account-wide limit (Dhan 1req/s, sqlite, config file) maar raha | #2, DH-904, ltp_poller se pehle ka LTP chaos |
| 7 | **Deploy drift / silent scp skip** — git me fix hai, VPS pe purana code chal raha; multi-file scp ne ek file chhod di | #27 #69 |
| 8 | **Hardcoded set/value vs canonical helper** — status-tuple/lot-size/expiry inline likha jabki helper/CSV maujood tha | #74, no-hardcode rule |
| 9 | **Mechanical edit, hot-loop regression** — find-replace/re-indent ne chalta loop chupchaap tod diya (syntax valid, semantics dead) | #56 #72 |
| 10 | **Report ka mechanism unverified** — bug report ne jo mechanism bataya wo deployed code pe possible hi nahi tha; asli gap ek line/branch aage tha (fix karne se pehle logs+code se verify karo) | #74 |
| 11 | **Cross-broker identifier translation** — string-guess mat karo; agar dono side structured fields dete hain (date/strike/enum), unhi se cross-match karo, formatted string kabhi wapas parse mat karo | #13, #79 (safe reverse-resolve pattern) |
| 12 | **Symptom ka fix wrong axis pe** — user/aap jo pehla fix sochte hain (granularity/display) wo aksar red herring hota hai; asli fix alag axis pe hota hai (yahan: time-confirmation, na ki number-size) — root cause trace kiye bina symptom ka pehla obvious fix mat lagao | kill-floor design (07-02) |

Fixed: `/api/close-position` now looks up the leg's `group_id` first and closes the whole group together if one exists — no single-leg-only path for a hedged pair remains in the UI. Same pass also found+fixed the TRAP #26 missing-`disclosedQuantity`/`afterMarketOrder` bug at 3 more independently-built raw-REST call sites (`/api/manual-order`, `/api/close-position`'s body, `/api/debug-test-order`) — none of them route through `DhanBroker.place_order()`, so the broker-class fix never covered them. Full writeup: **LESSONS.md TRAP #30** (risk + fix) and the TRAP #26 addendum (the 3 extra sites). **Still open, flagged not fixed:** hedge *placement* itself is best-effort (silent naked-leg-from-the-start if contract resolution/order fails), and there's no dashboard alert specifically for "this open position has no hedge." |

Fixed: sibling-close now uses the same 3-tier fallback, and a failure even past that queues the sibling in a new `_pending_group_close` dict, force-closed the very next monitor cycle (5s) the moment its price resolves — checked first, ahead of all other SL/TP/EOD logic. Full writeup: **LESSONS.md TRAP #30 addendum**. |

(b) **ITM immediate squareoff** — if a short option goes ITM on expiry day (`risk_gate.option_is_itm(trad_sym, spot)`, spot from `shared_ltp_cache` for NIFTY/BNF/FINNIFTY, REST for stocks), fires `EXPIRY_ITM_SQUAREOFF` immediately — no waiting for 2:55. (c) **No new entries after 2:00 PM on expiry day** — `range_trader.py` entry block checks `EXPIRY_NO_ENTRY_AFTER_HM=(14,0)` + `is_expiry_day(last_known_sec_id)`, uses `continue` to skip. All times/thresholds configurable in `risk_gate.py` constants. **LESSONS.md TRAP #36**. |

Fix: `smart_order.place_hedge_if_configured()` now always passes `product="NRML"` for hedge BUY; `range_trader` hedge call also passes `product="NRML"`. Both `KiteBroker` + `DhanBroker` `place_order()` accept `product` param (`"NRML"` → `PRODUCT_NRML`/`"MARGIN"`, else MIS/INTRADAY). `BaseBroker` abstract method signature updated. Since 3:15 EOD squareoff always fires, overnight risk = zero. (b) **Live P&L was recorded BEFORE broker confirmed fill** — `smart_order.execute()` used to log `[LIVE]` + `order_store.record()` immediately on order placement. Now: fire order → poll `broker.get_fill(order_id)` every 1.5s up to 8s (5 attempts) → `TRADED` = use actual `average_price`/`tradedPrice` for P&L, log `[LIVE]`, record; `REJECTED` = log `[LIVE-SKIP]`, `ok=False`, nothing recorded; timeout = log `[LIVE-PENDING]`, `ok=False`, nothing recorded. Both `DhanBroker` + `KiteBroker` now implement `get_fill(order_id) → (status, fill_price)`. Paper mode unchanged. Full writeup: **LESSONS.md TRAP #34 + TRAP #35**. |
| 2026-06-29 | **Trade-chart now shows the same zone/key-level overlay as the Watch-chart, on the underlying instrument pane (left, split-view) — plus 2 bugs found+fixed while building it, plus a real pivot-data bug found by comparing against TradingView.** (a) New `/api/trade-chart-underlying-data?strategy=` param reads `data/watch_<strategy>.json` for the underlying root symbol (same source the Watch-chart uses); `openTradeChart()` in `index.html` now threads `t.strategy` through every call-site (7 of them) and `chartTradeList`/`goTo()` nav too. (b) **Found+fixed:** the Watch-chart's box-mask trick (2 stacked Area series, one fills below "upper", a second re-fills below "lower" with background color) hides any REAL candle below the zone's lower bound — fine for the Watch-chart's live single-zone view, but trade-chart shows the FULL day, where price routinely trades both above and below a zone formed mid-session; a MARUTI trade's candles vanished almost entirely this way. Fixed: zone drawn as 2 plain dashed price-lines on this pane instead of a filled box — never hides candles. (c) **Found+fixed:** candles + grid + time-axis weren't rendering at all on the left pane (price-scale labels showed, canvas didn't) — `LightweightCharts.createChart()` measures the container's size at call time, and this pane's multi-level flex layout (split-wrap > pane > chart-left) sometimes hadn't finished resolving a height yet on first paint. Fixed: pass explicit `width`/`height` at creation + force a re-measure on the next 2 animation frames. (d) UI polish: Watchlist sidebar moved to the right (was left) and made collapsible (▶/◀ edge button); pane full-view toggle changed from Ctrl+Click to **Alt+Click** (was conflicting with browser/OS Ctrl shortcuts) and fixed to actually toggle back to split-view on a second Alt+Click (previously only Esc could restore it). (e) **CRITICAL — found while comparing LT's dashboard pivot levels against the same symbol's TradingView chart (user spotted R1/P/S1/PDH etc. were just plain wrong, ~30-50pts off):** `_CHARTING/zones.py`'s `build_key_levels()` hardcodes `iloc[-2]=yesterday, iloc[-1]=today` — true for the backtest tools (which deliberately slice data to end at their simulated day) but NOT guaranteed live, because Dhan's daily-historical endpoint never returns a partial "today" bar and was found to lag by 2+ trading days (LT's last available daily row was Thursday when "today" was Monday — Friday's row was simply missing). This silently aged EVERY symbol's pivot/PD_H/PD_C/PD_L levels by an extra stale day, in both chart display AND the live entry-signal logic (`daily_levels[symbol]` feeds `run_signal_engine()`'s zone-touch checks directly). Fixed in `range_trader.py`'s `main()` loop only (not the shared `zones.py` function, to avoid touching backtest tooling's correct behavior): pad `daily_df` with a dummy today-dated row whenever the last real row isn't actually today, before calling `build_key_levels()`. Verified the fix produces values matching TV (PDH 4272.3, P 4225.27, R1 4263.43 vs TV's eyeballed ~4270/~4225/~4263). ARS_CHAIN_V1 restarted live to load it (confirmed zero open positions via `order_store` first, not log tail). Also added (unrelated, same session): Engulfing/Harami/Inside-Candle plotshape markers to `_PINE/range_chain.pine` (previously only barcolor-tinted, easy to miss) — display-only, doesn't touch entry/exit conditions. Full writeup: **LESSONS.md TRAP #31**. |
| 2026-07-01 | **Peak-P&L day-rollover fix (TRAP #55) + a critical pos_monitor_loop silent-crash bug found while deploying it (TRAP #56).** User spotted stale `Peak ₹7,916 \| DD ₹7,916` + `30% floor ₹5,541` on a brand-new trading day with zero trades. Root cause: `pos_monitor_loop`'s day-rollover check compared `peak_pnl_history.json`'s own mtime against itself — but the file had just been rewritten a few lines earlier in the SAME loop iteration, so `_mdate` was always "today" and the archive/reset branch was permanently dead code. Since `trader_dashboard.py`/`monitor_daemon.py` run as long-lived systemd services (not restarted every trading day), `_trailing_peak_pnl`/`_daily_peak_ever` just carried forward unbounded across days. Fixed with an explicit module-level `_peak_day_str` tracker, checked BEFORE the file write, no longer mtime-dependent. **While deploying that fix, restarting `algo-monitor` exposed a separate, more serious pre-existing bug** from an earlier commit made ~10 min before this session (`3cbad3f`, a `datetime.utcnow()` Python-3.12-deprecation cleanup, co-authored by an earlier Claude session): the mechanical find-and-replace broke imports in 5 places. (1) 3 `risk_gate.py` functions (`_today_open`/`_today_realized_pnl`/`_strategy_day_pnl` — capital allocation, daily-loss breaker, concentration checks) lost their `import datetime`/`timedelta` entirely, throwing `NameError` on every call. (2) 2 spots in `trader_dashboard.py` — `_trailing_lock_fired_today()` + the trailing-lock flag-write inside `pos_monitor_loop` — had `from datetime import datetime as _dtc` mangled into `from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone as _dtc`; in a multi-name `from X import a, b, c as d`, `as` only renames the LAST name, so `_dtc` ended up bound to `timezone` (no `.now()` method) instead of `datetime`. The flag-write instance was the dangerous one: merely having that local import statement ANYWHERE inside `pos_monitor_loop`'s body makes Python treat `datetime` as local for the ENTIRE function (scoping, not execution order) — so the loop's very first line, `ist_now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)`, started throwing `UnboundLocalError` every single cycle. Both failures were completely invisible: `_trailing_lock_fired_today()` has a bare `except Exception: return False` (fails PERMISSIVE — the "block new entries after a profit-lock squareoff" flag silently stopped blocking anything), and `pos_monitor_loop`'s outer catch-all `print("Pos monitor error:", e)` is missing `flush=True`, so on a systemd service with block-buffered stdout the error sat unflushed for minutes with zero log signal that SL/TP/EOD-squareoff enforcement was entirely down. Bug had been dormant since the commit landed — only surfaced because a process reload was needed anyway. Fixed all 5 spots, verified `ast.parse` + a one-shot diagnostic script reproducing `pos_monitor_loop`'s exact call sequence, deployed, restarted both VPS services, confirmed clean (`peak_pnl_history.json` writing genuine `0.0` entries, zero errors in logs). No open positions during the incident window — zero live-trading impact this time, but the failure mode (SL/TP loop silently doing nothing) is exactly the scenario that matters most when a position IS open. Full writeups: **LESSONS.md TRAP #55 + TRAP #56**. |
| 2026-07-01 | **Restart-risk scenario modeling — 2 preemptive fixes (mode-preservation + untracked-position scan), deployed live.** User asked for a preemptive scenario analysis of what breaks when strategies/VPS get restarted (a common event — dev happens directly on the VPS). Modeled 4 restart types (single strategy process / `algo-monitor` / `algo-dashboard` / full VPS reboot) against 3 risk categories (data corruption / order-flow disruption / position mismatch), grounded in actual code reads (not guesswork) — 4 concrete findings, user chose to fix the top 2 now. **TRAP #57 — silent live→paper mode downgrade:** `auto_scheduler()`'s crash/restart-recovery path hardcoded `mode=paper` on every `/api/start` call; `nifty_config.json` never stored which mode a strategy was actually last running in. Two triggers land here: a VPS reboot (services are `systemctl enable`d, auto-start, but always in paper), and — found while testing — `algo-monitor` restarted anytime during 9:10–15:30 immediately re-runs the "start all active strategies" pass (`has_started_today` is a function-local var, resets on every fresh process start, not just a calendar-day change) — harmless no-op for already-running strategies (`/api/start` checks `get_pid()` first) but silently revives a crashed-while-LIVE strategy in PAPER. `health_check.py` doesn't check for this mismatch either. Fix: `/api/start` now persists `cfg[s]['mode']` alongside `active`; `auto_scheduler` reads it back instead of hardcoding paper. **TRAP #58 — untracked live position (the ghost-detection blind spot's mirror image):** `broker_sync.py`'s existing ghost-position sync (TRAP #44) only ever checked one direction — "order_store says OPEN, is broker actually flat?" — iterating positions SOURCED from order_store, so it can never catch the opposite case: a broker position that order_store has literally no row for. Root mechanism: `smart_order.execute()`'s live path has a real ~8s window (5×1.5s fill-confirm poll, by design per TRAP #35) between the broker accepting the order and `order_store.record()` running; a restart landing in that window kills the process via SIGTERM — and a codebase-wide grep confirmed **zero** `signal.signal()` handlers anywhere (only one place sends SIGTERM, `/api/stop`; nothing catches it) — so the order may fill at the broker while the DB row never gets written. Worst case: this orphan is the ONLY position that exists, so the old ghost-sync (gated on a non-empty known-open list) would never even query the broker to find it. Fix: new `broker_sync.untracked_scan_if_due()` (same 30s cadence, wired into `pos_monitor_loop` right next to the existing ghost-sync) — unconditionally polls both brokers' live positions via a new `positions_detailed()` method (added to `BaseBroker`/`DhanBroker`/`KiteBroker`, default `[]` so it degrades gracefully) and diffs against order_store's known-open set per broker. Dhan orphans auto-adopt into order_store (`status=open`, tagged `UNTRACKED_ADOPTED` — safe because Dhan's own position response includes its own tradingSymbol/segment directly, zero guessing) so `pos_monitor_loop` immediately starts applying default SL/EOD protection; Kite orphans are alert-only (never guess a Dhan trad_sym from a Kite tradingsymbol — same caution as TRAP #13/#22's forward-only `resolve_kite_symbol()`). Both write a red-banner alert to `downloader_alert.json` either way (`untracked_position` key, same convention as TRAP #53's naked-leg alert). **Scoped out for a later session (user's call):** webhook position recovery drops the profit-target (`target: None` on `_recover_wh_state()`, SL still restores fine) and `nifty_config.json` has no atomic-write protection against a concurrent-write/mid-write-kill corruption. Deployed to VPS, syntax-verified, restarted with zero open positions, confirmed clean for 2+ min (no errors, peak-P&L still healthy, untracked-scan runs silently since there was nothing to find — the actual "adopt a real orphan" code path is reviewed carefully but not yet live-exercised). Full writeups: **LESSONS.md TRAP #57 + TRAP #58**. |

Deployed + restarted live (with the user's explicit go-ahead at each step, given real capital was involved), independently verified via a read-only dry-run *before* the restart (confirmed the two real orphans as UNTRACKED, the one legitimately-tracked position as KNOWN) — then confirmed again live post-restart: both alerts written successfully. **RELIANCE-1310-CE remained open and genuinely unprotected at the point this session's investigation ended** (Kite orphans are alert-only by design — no auto-SL, unlike Dhan) — user chose to handle it manually on Zerodha directly rather than have a deeper structural fix rushed through live. **Deferred, not done:** the real fix for the root 8s-timeout gap — `smart_order.execute()` should write a `status="pending"` order_store row immediately on broker acceptance, then update it on confirmed fill, closing the gap at the source instead of relying on after-the-fact scanning. | 2026-07-01 | **TRAP #60 — ghost-sync duplicate-exit feedback loop, found live during a full-day P&L reconciliation with Zerodha, root-fixed.** After handling the RELIANCE orphan manually, user asked to fully sync the day's records against Zerodha ("Sync from Broker" only ever checks one direction — DB-open-but-broker-flat — so it can't catch this class of issue). Reconciling MARUTI's trade history surfaced a live, actively-running bug: `broker_sync._run_sync()` was writing a NEW duplicate "exit" record for MARUTI roughly every 30 seconds, all at the same stale price (₹388.00) — ~20 phantom rows accumulated during the diagnosis itself. Root cause, two compounding pre-existing gaps: (1) `_net_rows()`'s Pass-1 netting is a simple side-alternating pairing per (source,strategy,trad_sym) key in database-id order — for a symbol with an ODD count of same-key legs in a day, the last leg is always left "dangling" as open in the netting engine's view, even when the day is genuinely flat at the broker; (2) `_fetch_fills()` collapsed ALL of a symbol's fills for the day into a single dict entry (last-write-wins), so `_resolve_exit_price()` had no way to distinguish "a fill I already used to close something" from "a genuinely new, unrecorded fill" — it just handed back whatever the most recent cached price was, every time. Combined: every ~30s cycle, gap (1) flagged something as dangling, gap (2) handed back the same stale price, and `_run_sync` wrote another synthetic exit — which itself became a new row that could flip which leg looked dangling on the NEXT pass, so the loop perpetuated instead of self-correcting. This precisely explains why it only surfaced today: TRAP #58/#59's fixes (correct `resolve_kite_symbol` signature + correct `quantity` field name) meant `_check_flat()` could, for the first time, correctly and repeatedly confirm "yes, broker is flat" — triggering this dormant weakness on every single cycle instead of failing silently as it always had before. Not a live-trading-risk bug (MARUTI was genuinely flat at the broker throughout) — purely a bookkeeping/data-integrity corruption, but one that actively degrades trust in every number the dashboard shows and could misattribute a real still-open position under different timing. **Root fix:** `_fetch_fills()` now carries each fill's own unique broker id (Kite's native `trade_id`; `exchangeTradeId`/`orderId` newly added to `DhanBroker.trades()`, which never exposed one before) alongside price. Before writing any exit record, `_run_sync` now checks `_fill_already_used(tid, trad_sym, broker_name)` — a lookup against `order_store`'s `correlation_id` field (already existed in the schema, just never populated for this purpose) — and skips entirely (no duplicate write, and critically no `mark_externally_closed()` either, since that would silently drop a legitimate row's leg from all P&L via `_dead_filtered()`) if that exact fill was already consumed on an earlier cycle. **Manual data correction** (this incident only, not a recurring task): backed up `trades.db`, deleted ~20 phantom/duplicate MARUTI rows, inserted 4 clean verified round-trip pairs derived directly from Kite's own `trades()` fills — cross-validated against RELIANCE/SUNPHARMA-1880/SUNPHARMA-1980-hedge, all of which matched Zerodha's own per-instrument P&L exactly via independent net-cashflow calculation, confirming the reconciliation method before trusting it for MARUTI. Final day total: ₹1,827.50, exact match with Zerodha, `open: []` confirmed stable across two separate 60-second monitoring windows (once before the root fix, once after, to isolate "did my manual cleanup hold" from "did the root fix actually stop new phantoms"). Deployed + restarted with zero open positions. Full writeup: **LESSONS.md TRAP #60**. |

Root trigger (**TRAP #62**, not fixed this session): the account-level trailing-profit-lock in `pos_monitor_loop` (which squares off the whole account once P&L drops enough from peak — separate from any single strategy's own exit logic) closed a SUNPHARMA short at 10:26, but never told the owning strategy process (`range_trader.py`); its in-memory `_state` kept believing the position was still open for the next 40 minutes, until its own ATR-trailing-stop condition fired and it placed a real BUY order to "close" a position that no longer existed — a genuine unintended trade (~₹122.50 cost). `_recover_state_from_order_store()` (TRAP #28) already handles this exact class of gap for restarts; there's no equivalent live-resync while the process keeps running. Flagged with two possible fix directions (push a signal on squareoff, or have the strategy periodically self-validate against order_store) for a future session. **TRAP #61 (fixed):** investigating that phantom BUY exposed a second, independent, pre-existing bug from TRAP #44's original 2026-06-29 design — `broker_sync._run_sync()` called `mark_externally_closed()` on the entry row UNCONDITIONALLY after a ghost-close, even when it had just successfully recorded a proper pairing exit leg. Since that status is in `_dead_filtered()`'s exclusion set, the entry vanished from all P&L (open and closed both) while its exit leg — correctly recorded, TRAP #60's fix working exactly as designed — was left with no partner, displaying as a fresh unmatched "open position" instead of the completed round-trip it actually was. This bug had existed the whole time but only surfaced today because every earlier ghost-close this session hit TRAP #59 or #60 first. Fixed: `mark_externally_closed()` now only fires in the genuine no-exit-price-found case; when an exit WAS recorded, the entry's status is left alone and normal Pass-2 FIFO netting pairs it naturally. One row's status manually reverted to re-pair the existing data. Final day total: ₹1,705.00 — exact match with Zerodha's own "Total P&L" figure. Full writeups: **LESSONS.md TRAP #61 + TRAP #62**. |
| 2026-07-01 | **TRAP #63 — TRAP #58's root cause fixed at the source, requested by user after the same gap recurred 4 times in one session.** `smart_order.execute()`'s live path only wrote to `order_store` AFTER confirming `TRADED` within an 8s poll (5×1.5s) — timing out meant the position simply never got recorded, even though the broker fill was genuine. New `order_store.update_fill()` then corrects it once the poll resolves: `TRADED` → real price, tag cleared; `REJECTED` → `status="rejected"` (correctly excluded from all P&L via `_dead_filtered`); timeout → row left as-is, already protected by `pos_monitor_loop`'s SL/EOD like any other open leg, and self-healing via `broker_sync` either way (genuinely filled → nothing to do; genuinely never filled → found flat with no matching fill price, cleanly excluded via TRAP #61's no-exit-price branch). Doesn't relax TRAP #35's "never record an unconfirmed price" goal — the price still gets corrected to the real fill once known, this just stops treating "can't confirm within 8s" as "doesn't exist." Deployed while a real MARUTI position was open (SL-protected) — required restarting the `ARS_CHAIN_V1` strategy process specifically, since Python doesn't hot-reload an already-imported module; `_recover_state_from_order_store()` (TRAP #28) correctly re-attached the open position afterward with zero protection gap, verified via the dashboard immediately post-restart. Full writeup: **LESSONS.md TRAP #63**. |

| 2026-07-03 | **Per-position Trailing Points (premium) SL spike-guard + the "two RSI files" confusion permanently closed (TRAP #83-84).** User asked for a review of the "Trailing Points (premium)" default SL (Gemini-built) before trusting it with real capital, plus why yesterday's RSI session showed a symbol re-bought right after it was sold, paired with ₹0 phantom P&L rows. **TRAP #83 (fixed):** `_pos_monitor_check_one`'s `MAX_LTP`/`MIN_LTP` ratcheted off a single raw tick with no confirmation — unlike the KILL-ALL floor's own 2-reading confirmed-peak (built specifically because a spike tick can inflate/misfire a trailing lock, TRAP #80-81) — so one bad/stale tick could permanently ratchet a position's SL to a level real price never actually held, then get hit on the next candle close. Fixed by adding a SEPARATE `CONF_MAX_LTP`/`CONF_MIN_LTP`/`PREV_LTP` track (same `confirmed = min/max(prev, cur)` technique as `risk_gate.advance_trailing_lock`) feeding only the `trailing_pt` SL/TP ratchet in `_generic_px` — the existing raw `MAX_LTP`/`MIN_LTP` tags are untouched, still driving the Run-Up/Run-Down display as before (that one's supposed to show the real best/worst tick, glitch or not). Flagged but left alone per user's explicit call (Target Type isn't set to Trailing Points): the same trailing_pt branch's TP side has `ref_ltp` bound to the wrong extreme (min_ltp used for a BUY's TP instead of max_ltp) — dormant today, would misbehave if Target Type is ever switched to Trailing Points. **TRAP #84 (fixed):** the dashboard's `STRATEGIES` dict runs `_TRADERS/01_rsi_v1.py` for BOTH `"rsi"` and `"rsi_v1"` — but 2026-07-02's TRAP #76 restart-recovery fix landed in `_TRADERS/rsi_trader.py`, a separate, never-actually-run duplicate file. Direct read of `01_rsi_v1.py` confirmed zero restart-recovery: any mid-day restart wiped its in-memory `positions`/`active_opts` to flat while a real position could still be open, and the next matching signal fired a duplicate BUY on an already-open symbol — `order_store._net_rows()`'s Pass-1 netting then treats that as a "pyramid/dup", orphans the original leg into `leftover`, and Pass-2 FIFO-pairs it by symbol alone (blind to strategy/causality) — producing exactly the same-price ₹0 ghost round-trips seen live on NTPC/HDFCBANK. Fixed by porting `_recover_rsi_state()` (ported from the unused file, with its "closed" key bug corrected to "details" — a latent undercounting bug in the original that never actually ran) into `01_rsi_v1.py`, seeding `last_date` to today (not `None`, per TRAP #76's exact lesson) right after recovery, plus a NEW per-cycle order_store re-validation (not just at startup) so an external close mid-day — manual, trailing-lock squareoff, pos_monitor EOD — clears stale in-memory state before the next signal, not just at the next restart. **Root-caused why the confusion kept recurring:** `_TOOLS/backtest_engine.py` had `import rsi_trader as rsit` (only for its 6-line `compute_rsi()`) — `01_rsi_v1.py` can't be imported as a module (Python identifiers can't start with a digit), so the backtester's only usable import target for RSI math was the unused duplicate, which is exactly why it kept looking "real" enough to survive past reviews. Fixed by inlining the same Wilder-RSI formula directly into `backtest_engine.py` (`_compute_rsi()`, no cross-file dependency on either strategy script), which let `_TRADERS/rsi_trader.py` be deleted outright with zero other callers (verified via repo-wide grep — every remaining hit was a comment/docstring). Also fixed the underlying `STRATEGIES["rsi"]["grep"]` mismatch (`"rsi_trader"` → `"01_rsi_v1"`, `trader_dashboard.py`) and the matching duplicate-target entry in `health_check.py`'s own `TRADER_SCRIPTS["rsi"]` — both harmless in practice (grep is only a PID-detection fallback behind the `--id` exact-token match) but the same root confusion pattern. CLAUDE.md's Files table + Critical Rule 6 updated (both referenced the now-deleted file / a since-fixed "RMS-blind" claim for rsi_v1). **Deployed same session** — pushed to GitHub, scp'd to VPS, `algo-dashboard`+`algo-monitor` restarted (zero open positions confirmed twice, market not yet open — safest possible window), logs verified clean post-restart. Full writeups: **LESSONS.md TRAP #83-84**. |

Root cause: both candle-fetchers discard any data older than today (`01_rsi_v1.py` never even requests a prior day from Dhan; `range_trader.py` requests 2 days back but then filters down to today-only), so bar-count restarts from zero every market open — and both have a hard minimum-bar gate before any signal: Chain needs 20 bars × 1m ≈ 9:35 AM (`range_trader.run_signal_engine()` line 300), RSI needs 19 bars × 2m ≈ 9:53 AM (`01_rsi_v1.get_signal()`). Fixed by excluding `CAPITAL_BLOCKED`-tagged rows in `risk_gate._today_open()` and all 3 strategies' recovery functions (`range_trader.py`/`universe_trader.py`/`01_rsi_v1.py`) — deliberately did NOT touch `order_store._dead_filtered()` itself, since the dashboard's own "🚫 Capital se Block hui Entries" display legitimately needs these rows to keep showing there. | 2026-07-03 | **Exit-reason tagging fix (range_trader's own ATR-trailing exits were showing blank) + dhan_feed contention permanently closed (TRAP #88), user escalated as priority.** (a) User asked why a completed TCS trade's "Exit Reason" column was blank — traced to `range_trader.py` computing+logging a reason (`"ATR_TRAILING"`) but never tagging it on the actual exit order; only `pos_monitor_loop`'s own SL/TP/EOD exits tagged themselves. (b) TRAP #87's backoff reduced the hammering but didn't clear the underlying 429s — still rejected every attempt even at the 30s cap, 10+ minutes straight. User escalated: "ye meri main strategy hai... entry kaise milegi." Root-caused properly this time: `monitor_daemon.py` (the actual SL/TP/EOD/RMS safety process) never calls `dhan_feed.start()` at all — it gets LTP entirely from `ltp_poller.py`'s separate REST-based poller — so `algo-dashboard`'s own feed connection was pure UI-freshness (every consumer already has a REST fallback) and was just permanently contending with `ARS_CHAIN_V1`'s connection for the same account's slot. Disabled `_ensure_feed_started()` in `trader_dashboard.py` entirely — `range_trader.py` is now the only process opening a feed connection. Restarted all 3 processes (zero real positions confirmed before each step); feed connected clean on the first attempt post-restart, zero 429s in 20+ seconds of observation (vs. continuous rejections before). Full writeups: **LESSONS.md TRAP #88**. |

Re-enabled `algo-dashboard`'s own feed call now that it's safe with any number of callers. Verified with a standalone 5-case simulated-multi-process test before deploying to live money; post-deploy `algo-monitor` won the race, `range_trader.py` correctly waited — zero errors, real AXISBANK position correctly recovered through the restart. Verified against real numbers before deploying (`dhan_real_margin()` on the actual open position, real Zerodha `funds()` call showing ~₹9.1L available) — deliberately left the manually-configured ₹1-2L cap unchanged (user's own words: that's a paper-trading ceiling, still the actual day-to-day constraint since it's currently more restrictive than real funds; this fix is the safety net underneath it, not a replacement). Full writeup: **LESSONS.md TRAP #90**. |

| 2026-07-03 | **TRAP #92's phantom trade cascaded into a real KILL-FLOOR false-fire (blocked ALL new entries for the rest of the day) + a second independent double-counting bug in "Reconcile vs Broker" for NIFTY/BANKNIFTY (TRAP #93).** Same-day follow-up: user reported the Peak P&L graph still showing the corrupted ₹15.77L peak even after TRAP #92's DB cleanup, plus a second duplicate row (`NIFTY2670724350CE`, tagged `manual`). (1) The account-wide MTM/peak tracker had already computed and PERSISTED the fake peak into `peak_pnl_history.json`/`kill_floor_state.json` while the bug was live — KILL-FLOOR genuinely fired off that fake number (`MTM ₹2111 stayed below floor ₹1576172`), writing the day's lock-fired flag and blocking all new entries account-wide. Deleting the underlying DB rows didn't fix it: the peak is an intentional monotonic "never resets" high-water-mark reloaded from disk on every restart. First correction attempt got silently undone — edited the files while the OLD `algo-monitor` process was still running, and its own next write cycle (every ~5s) re-corrupted the file with its still-stale in-memory peak before the restart even took effect. Redone correctly: `systemctl stop` first (confirmed no writer alive), corrected ~350 corrupted history entries + reset `kill_floor_state.json` (`armed:false` — the real ₹1,838 total is well under the ₹6,000 arm threshold) + deleted the fired-flag file, THEN `systemctl start` — verified `risk_gate.kill_floor_fired_today()` returns `False` and a fresh restart now shows `Restored peak ₹2619`. (2) Separately: `broker_sync._reconcile_sig()`'s regex-based strike extraction works for monthly stock options (letter month-code separates expiry from strike) but swallows the WHOLE digit run for weekly index options with no separator (`"NIFTY2670724350CE"` parsed strike as `"2670724350"`, not `"24350"`) — meaning the signature never matched the real trade already recorded in Dhan format, so **every past NIFTY/BANKNIFTY reconcile has likely double-counted** a real trade as a separate "manual" one. Fixed by resolving Kite's raw symbol to Dhan's canonical format via `KiteBroker.resolve_dhan()` (the same safe structured-field resolver from TRAP #79) before computing the signature — tested against the real scrip master, confirmed matching. Also fixed `_UI_CONFIG` leaking into the Logs tab sidebar (missing from an exclusion filter). All DB writes (row deletions, state-file corrections) done only after explicit user confirmation, re-verifying exact row content immediately before each write. Full writeups: **LESSONS.md TRAP #93**. |

| 2026-07-03 | **DH-904 rate-limit architecture overhaul (TRAP #95) + Rate Limit Room moved RMS → Logs tab.** User asked why timeouts flood the Rate Limit Room all day and whether the cache/batching architecture actually works. Found 5 compounding gaps: (1) `_last_closed_candle_close()` threw a naive-vs-aware datetime error on EVERY call — AFTER making an UNTHROTTLED Dhan candle call and BEFORE writing its cache, so each pos_monitor tick burned an invisible raw call (the journal error line had been there all along); (2) ltp_poller/strategies/`_rest_ltp_fallback` all ignored `acquire()`'s False and posted to Dhan anyway during cooldowns, extending them; (3) no per-endpoint budgets — Dhan 429s `/marketfeed/ltp` and `/charts/*` independently (~1/sec each), confirmed empirically when post-candle-fix 429s were ALL ltp-priority; new `PRIORITY_SUBCAP={"candle":1,"ltp":1}` (pwindows table, unit-tested 1+1+1 per window); (4) `Dashboard:IdxLTP`/`Dashboard:PosLTP` never read the shared_ltp_cache that ltp_poller warms every 1.5s with exactly their sec_ids — now cache-first; (5) `unknown ×178` in the event log — poller/monitor never `set_context()`, misdirecting diagnosis; now fully tagged. Candle caching made bar-boundary-aware (a fetch after the current bar opened contains every closed bar — lossless reuse). 1000-symbol batching: LTP already batched via ltp_poller; Dhan has NO multi-symbol candle endpoint, hence cache+smoothing instead. Deployed dashboard+monitor immediately (md5-verified); strategy processes NOT restarted mid-day (4 real open positions, changes defensive — inherit at next scheduled start). Immediate result: tz-error zero, "unknown" eliminated, candle 429s zero. Full writeup: **LESSONS.md TRAP #95**. |
| 2026-07-03 | **TRAP #95 part 2/3 — rolling-window min-spacing + 2 more untracked callers + poller dynamic warm-list.** Post-deploy 5-min measurement proved remaining 429s were ALL ltp-priority (Dhan limits /marketfeed/ltp separately). (a) The sub-cap was per-calendar-second counting — blind to Dhan's ROLLING window (a call at N.95s + one at N+1.05s = "2 in 100ms" to Dhan). (b) Process audit found 2 more Dhan callers on the shared account: `auto_data_downloader.py` (systemd data-downloader) bypassed the limiter — wired in + note_429 on DH-904, deployed+restarted; `/root/order_book_deploy` (cron 09:12, separate project) already self-throttles 1/sec — left as-is. (c) `ltp_poller.request_watch()` dynamic warm-list (90s TTL, file-backed): dashboard positions-LTP/option-LTP routes now ask the ONE batched poller to keep their sec_ids warm instead of each REST-calling; PosLTP returns `cache-pending` when the gate's busy (frontend re-polls 3s). This is the real "dashboard overwhelm na kare" fix — all repeating dashboard price needs funnel into the poller's single call/cycle. Deployed dashboard+monitor (market closed, clean restart). Full writeup: **LESSONS.md TRAP #95 Part 2/3**. |
